      - id: AC-001
        text: On Linux with Landlock, a missing export root (with its missing ancestors) and missing nested export folders are created on a thread Landlock-confined to the pinned root or, for a missing root, its deepest existing ancestor (pinned from its approved mount without following links or crossing mounts); a parent moved outside before mkdirat is refused by the kernel and nothing appears outside (BUG-BASELINE-001; BugTriage139EighteenthLinuxTests EnsureDirectory_MissingNestedDirectory_IsCreatedUnderLandlock, EnsureDirectory_ParentMovedOutsideBeforeCreate_IsDeniedByLandlock, OpenExportRoot_MissingRootAndAncestors_IsCreatedUnderLandlock, LandlockConfinement_DeniesDirectoryCreationOutsideItsHierarchy).
        isSatisfied: false
      - id: AC-002
        text: On Linux with Landlock, nested stale files and empty directories are deleted on a thread Landlock-confined to the root through the pinned parent after the target kind and identity match; a missing parent or target returns false; a parent moved outside before unlinkat is refused by the kernel and the entry survives; export cleanup deletes nested stale files (BUG-BASELINE-001; DeleteFileIfExists_NestedStaleFile_DeletesUnderLandlock, DeleteFileIfExists_NestedParentMovedOutsideBeforeUnlink_IsDeniedByLandlock, RequirementsExport_NestedStaleCleanup_DeletesStaleUnderLandlock).
        isSatisfied: false
      - id: AC-003
        text: 'On Linux without Landlock the export fails closed as before: missing roots and nested folders throw DirectoryNotFoundException before any mutation and nested stale cleanup is refused before any generated write (BUG-BASELINE-001; EnsureDirectory_MissingNestedDirectory_FailsClosedBeforeMutation, OpenExportRoot_MissingRoot_FailsClosedWithoutLandlock, RequirementsExport_NestedStaleCleanup_IsRejectedBeforeGeneratedWrite).'
        isSatisfied: false
      - id: AC-004
        text: If the Landlock confinement cannot be established (ruleset creation, the path-beneath rule, no_new_privs, or restrict_self fails), the confined operation never runs and the call fails with an IOException, so no export mutation happens without kernel enforcement (BUG-BASELINE-001 what-if; BugTriage139EighteenthLinuxTests.LandlockConfinement_RuleSetupFailure_NeverRunsTheOperation, where the kernel rejects directory rights on a regular-file descriptor).
        isSatisfied: false

      description: >-
        Resolve and validate physical filesystem identity through interruptible native handles or isolated workers whose public deadlines and accounting remain truthful; bind SQLite opening to the validated object without lexical races; attribute PostgreSQL cancellation to the actual caller cancel request; compose platform-native tests and process containment without pass-through fallbacks; and generate exact-final-HEAD evidence through a mechanically verified immutable workflow.

        text: "The SQLite caller-side data-source preflight runs off the caller's thread under the open's single finite deadline and the caller's cancellation: OpenAsync returns its task at once, a preflight that does not return fails the open with TimeoutException at the bound, no open worker starts, and the preflight stays counted (ActivePreflightCount) until it actually returns (BUG-BASELINE-009 C2; SqliteBoundedOpenFourteenthTests.OpenAsync_PreflightHeldAtBoundary_TimesOutWithoutBlockingTheCaller)."
        isSatisfied: false
      - id: AC-003
        text: Linux workspace identity resolution resolves symbolic-link prefixes through the mount table before its anchored RESOLVE_NO_XDEV lookup, so an absolute alias within a nested approved mount (for example /tmp) resolves like the data-file and SQLite opens instead of failing with a mount transition (BUG-BASELINE-009 C3; WorkspaceIdentityFourteenthNativeTests.NativeResolver_RepeatedPlatformCallsLeaveNoActiveWorkers).
        isSatisfied: false
      - id: AC-004
        text: 'A bounded traversal or file worker that uses the export root descriptor holds a reference on it from before it starts until it actually exits, so disposing the root at the public deadline defers the close: on Linux the root descriptor stays open while the worker runs and closes after it exits; on Windows the open root still blocks rename (BUG-BASELINE-009, operator decision 2026-10-11 "Fix it now"; BugTriage139EighteenthTraversalTests.EnumerateFilesBoundedAsync_BlockingMetadata_TimesOutWithTruthfulWorkerAccounting).'
        isSatisfied: false

    - id: TR-MCP-VOICE-003
      title: Voice Session Lifecycle Management
      description: >-
        One active session per device enforced via `DeviceId` lookup; creating a new session for a device with an active session returns the existing session.     - id: TR-MCP-WS-003
      title: Workspace Process Manager
      description: >-
        Manages workspace marker file lifecycle. On startup, generates tokens and writes `AGENTS-README-FIRST.yaml` marker files for all registered workspaces - all pointing to the single shared host port. On stop, removes marker files. No longer spawns child `WebApplication` instances (replaced by single-app multi-tenant model, see TR-MCP-MT-001 through TR-MCP-MT-003).

    - id: TR-MCP-WS-004
      title: Workspace Controller
      description: >-
        REST API at `/mcpserver/workspace` with Base64URL-encoded path keys. Provides create, read, update, delete, init, start, stop, status, and prompt (GET/PUT) endpoints. All `/mcpserver/*` routes protected by `WorkspaceAuthMiddleware` (per-workspace token).

    - id: TR-MCP-WS-005
      title: Marker File Service
      description: >-
        `MarkerFileService.WriteMarkerAsync` writes `AGENTS-README-FIRST.yaml` to the workspace root. All markers point to the same shared host port. Uses Handlebars.Net templating with full workspace context. The marker template MUST be loaded from `templates/prompt-templates.yaml` via `PromptTemplateService` (id: `default-marker-prompt`). **CRITICAL**: If the template cannot be loaded (missing file, invalid YAML, or missing ID), the service must log a critical error and shut down the server immediately. Fallback to hardcoded templates is PROHIBITED. The YAML file contains port, `baseUrl`, all endpoint paths, process PID, `startedAt` timestamp, workspace name, per-workspace auth token (`apiKey`), and a machine-readable `prompt` block. Agents should send `X-Workspace-Path` header for workspace targeting.

        **Covered by:** FR: FR-MCP-018

      description: >-

      title: Workspace Auto-Init and Auto-Start on Creation
      description: >-
        `WorkspaceController` POST calls `WorkspaceService.InitAsync` to scaffold the directory structure, then calls `WorkspaceProcessManager.StartAsync` to bring the host online, all within a single request, before returning 201 Created.

    - id: TR-MCP-WS-009
      title: Primary Workspace Detection and IsEnabled Gating
      description: >-
        `WorkspaceProcessManager.IHostedService.StartAsync` resolves the primary workspace: first by `IsPrimary = true` + lowest port among enabled workspaces; then by lowest-port enabled workspace if none is marked primary. For the primary workspace, only a marker file is written - no child `WebApplication` is created. Workspaces with `IsEnabled = false` are skipped during auto-start but can be started manually.
